### Page 2

世界経済：AI・防衛関連需要を受け景況感は好調。一方でインフレ圧力の広がりも確認
 9月のグローバル製造業PMIは53.0（8月52.3）と堅調。非製造業は54.5（8月53.6）と40か月ぶりの高水準
― 製造業はAI・防衛関連投資需要を受け生産、新規受注ともに上昇。非製造業も幅広い業種で業況が改善
― 一方、仕入・販売価格指数はともに上昇。エネルギーや輸送価格上昇によるインフレ圧力の広がりを示唆
 国・地域別では米国・ユーロ圏が製造業・非製造業ともに改善。とりわけ米国の好調が目立つ
― 米国ではAI関連支出の拡大に伴い機械・設備投資および生産が増加。非製造業においても情報通信業が突出して好調
― ユーロ圏でも、AI投資に支えられたIT関連サービスが特に力強く拡大。専門・商業サービスも改善
1
製造業の景況感（購買担当者景気指数） 非製造業の景況感（購買担当者景気指数）
（注） 日米欧：S&P Global、中：国家統計局。購買担当者景気指数：企業に対するアン
ケート調査結果から作成した指数。新規受注や生産高、雇用、出荷遅延などの指数

に一定のウエイトを掛けて算出。50が景気拡大・縮小の分岐点
（出所）S&P Global、中国国家統計局より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
（注） 日米欧：S&P Global、中：国家統計局。購買担当者景気指数（非製造業）：企業に対
するアンケート調査結果から作成した指数。50が景気拡大・縮小の分岐点

### Page 3

金融市場概況：投資家心理が悪化し、日米とも株安・金利低下
 AIインフラ投資への信用不安の高まりから、日米株価はハイテク株を中心に下落
― 米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格急落が株安の契機に。原油高に伴うインフレ懸念も下押し圧力に
― 投資家心理が悪化するなかで、安全資産とされる日米国債に資金が流入、前月までの金利上昇基調は一巡
 ドル円は、1ドル＝150円台後半で方向感の乏しい展開
― 日米金利とも低下し、日米金利差は概ね横ばい圏で推移
― 原油高が輸入増を通じて円安要因となる一方、日米株安に伴うリスクオフや仏情勢悪化に伴うユーロ安は円高要因に
2

米国：インフレ率は高止まり、油価再加速で当面は物価高が続く懸念
 原油高や関税の影響から物価は加速
―9月のコアCPIは前月比＋0.3％（年率＋4.1％）と8月（前月比＋0.3％、年率＋3.5％）から伸びが拡大
― コアサービスでは住居費や輸送サービスなどが押し上げに寄与。コア財については、衣料品や玩具などが上昇。関税引
き上げに伴う価格転嫁が続いている模様
 足元で原油価格が再加速していることを踏まえれば、当面は物価の上振れリスクが残存する懸念
― シカゴ連銀が公表する週次の小売物価指標は9月後半から10月初めにかけて上昇しており、物価の高止まりを示唆
3
（出所） 米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
項目別コアCPI 週次小売物価（シカゴ連銀）

（出所） シカゴ連銀より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：足元の消費はガソリン高や関税に伴う物価上昇で足踏みも、年末商戦は堅調な見通し
 消費は足踏み。ガソリン価格高騰や関税に伴う物価高が家計の購買力を下押し
―9月の実質小売売上高は前月比▲0.5％と減少。ガソリン小売が減少したほか、自動車や衣料品も低調に推移
 物価高は逆風も、2026年の年末商戦は堅調予想
― 米マスターカードは、実質小売売上高（除くガソリン・自動車）は前年比＋2.7％と、前年（同＋2.1％）から伸びが拡大する
と予想。雇用環境の改善などを背景に幅広い所得層で安定した消費がみられると指摘
4
（注） ガソリンはCPIを用いて実質化。その他は、8、9月のみ各品目に対応するCPIを用

米国：雇用は減速も、AI関連需要が下支え
 9月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋2.9万人と市場予想（同＋9.0万人）を下回る結果。7月・8月も下方修正
― 民間部門が前月差＋4.6万人（8月同＋8.9万人）と増勢が鈍化したほか、政府部門が同▲1.7万人と再びマイナス転化
― 民間部門では、旺盛なAI投資需要などを背景に、製造や建設、建築・エンジニアリングなどが増加基調を維持したもの
の、情報、金融、専門サービスなどの高賃金セクターが減少
― 時間当たり賃金の伸びは前月比＋0.1％（8月同＋0.3％）と鈍化。こちらも市場予想（同＋0.3％）を下回る結果
― 総じて冴えない結果ながら、3カ月平均では＋5万人程度を維持しており、雇用は減速しつつも失速には至らず

5
（出所） 米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 7

米国：対人型業種の雇用に底堅さ。労働参加率上昇で失業率は小幅上昇
 対人接触度の高い業種では雇用に底堅さ、AIによる代替が進みにくい可能性
― ヘルスケアや建設、飲食サービスなど、対人接触機会の多い業種では雇用が増加する一方、経理・会計サービス、ITシ
ステム開発などでは低調
 失業率は4.2％と小幅悪化も引き続き低水準を維持
― 主に16-24歳の若年層およびプライムエイジ層の労働参加率の上昇が押し上げに寄与した模様
6
（注）1.バブルの大きさは各産業の雇用者数
2. 対人接触度は、O*NETの職業別のPhysical Proximity（業務上、他者と物理的に近
接する度合い）を、各産業の職業別雇用者構成で加重平均した指数
3.コロナ禍からの雇用回復の影響を除くため、雇用者数の変化率は2023年末を起点
に算出
（出所） 米労働省、セントルイス連銀より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：生産は底堅く推移。AI・防衛が投資財生産をけん引、半導体増産も寄与
 2026年8月の鉱工業生産は前年比＋1.4％と底堅い結果
― 半導体関連の生産が引き続きけん引。生産能力が2022年対比1.7倍に拡大するなど生産体制の増強が背景。事実、主
要半導体メーカーによる工場新設・増強を受け、MOSロジック（GPU、CPUなど演算用）の量産が本格化
 AI・防衛分野を中心に投資財生産も堅調さを維持
― 投資関連財はAI・防衛が押し上げる形で同＋4.7％と増勢継続。国防・宇宙関連設備は同＋6.1％ と全体を上回る伸び。
地政学リスクの高まりを背景に軍用機やミサイル・防空システムなどの生産体制強化が図られている模様
7

生活費
期待より悪い 期待通り 期待より良い
（シェア）
米国：中間選挙を目前に住宅市場は軟調、アフォーダビリティへの不満が共和党への逆風に
 住宅ローン金利は足元で再び上昇
― 借入コストの負担増により、足元の住宅ローン申請件数は減少
 物価上昇が生活コストを押し上げるなか、金利上昇による住宅取得環境の悪化も家計の不満を助長する要因に
― トランプ大統領の生活費への政策対応は約7割が「期待より悪い」と評価しており、支持率は低下基調
―11月に控える中間選挙では、アフォーダビリティへの対応が引き続き主要な論点

8

欧州：G7は原油・ディーゼルの協調放出で合意。米国の輸出制限リスクは後退
9
 G7はIEAを通じ4カ月で1億バレルの石油備蓄放出に合意。初期20日間はディーゼルを前倒し放出
― 背景にディーゼル価格高騰。中間選挙を控えたトランプ大統領は、禁輸も辞さない姿勢を強調（米国は世界需要の4.9％
を供給）。米国からの輸出が欧州の需要を支える一方、米国よりも潤沢な在庫を保有する欧州に圧力をかけた格好
― 欧州は石油製品消費に占めるディーゼル比率が高く、価格高騰を回避する思惑も協調放出の合意を後押し
― 合意ではG7域内でのエネルギー輸出制限回避も確認され、米国によるEU向け禁輸リスクはひとまず後退
石油製品の製品別消費割合の国際比較
（出所）Energy Institute より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
EUの石油製品消費はディーゼル・軽油が中心

### Page 11

欧州：ディーゼル需給逼迫は残存。欧州のインフレ上振れ要因に
10
 ロシアのディーゼル輸出は足元で急減。中東からの輸出減、中国の輸出停止も重なり、世界需要の約6％が失われた格好
―G7の1億バレル放出で当面の供給不安は緩和するものの、構造的な供給不足の解消には至らず
―IEAも、中東からの原油輸出は回復する一方、石油製品の供給制約はなお深刻と指摘。ディーゼル需給の逼迫が続け
ば、輸送・物流コストを通じたインフレ圧力が残存
 欧州では9月HICPが再加速。ディーゼル高が輸送・物流コストを通じて追加的なインフレ上振れ要因となるリスク
―9月消費者物価（HICP）は前年比＋3.8％（前月：同＋3.2％）と伸び加速。エネルギー価格の上昇が主因
今般の備蓄放出量と停止中のディーゼル輸出
（注） 世界需要およびロシア・中国の輸出量は2025年実績。中東は2026年8月実績を参
考に、輸出量を前年の4分の1としてシェアを概算
（出所）EIA、IEA、OPEC、各種報道より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（出所）Eurostat より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：EU・中国はハイブリッド車の輸入制限で暫定合意。同様の措置は他の品目に広がる可能性
11
 EUと中国は、貿易不均衡の是正措置について暫定合意。中国からのハイブリッド車輸入は従来見通しの半分以下に
―EUが中国産EVに追加関税を賦課した2024年以降、追加関税の対象とならないハイブリッド車輸入が急増
― 欧州委員会の試算では、今回の措置によって2030年までの4年間で数百万台の輸入を削減見込み。直近では、EUが輸
入するハイブリッド車の約半分を中国車が占めており、域内自動車市場に一定のインパクト
 欧州委員会は、幅広い産業で貿易不均衡を是正する必要性を示唆。同様の措置が他の品目にも広がる可能性
― 欧州委員会のモニタリング対象（輸入増加と価格下落が併存する品目）は、中国からの輸入品が最多

欧州：2027年度予算審議が本格化。生活苦によるデモが拡大する中、財政赤字は高水準
12
 2027年度予算審議が本格化。財政赤字は総じて高止まり
― 一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連支出を免除する特例措置の適用後も、複数の国でEU財政基準で定める赤字幅を超過。

― フランスの学生によるデモを発端に、生活費の高騰や公共サービスの劣化に対する抗議活動は欧州各国に拡大。一部

欧州：長期金利上昇を受け、一部ECB高官は追加利上げの必要性低下を指摘
13
 フランスでは国内政治・財政面の不安から長期金利が上昇。イタリア・ギリシャなど周辺の高債務国にも波及
― フランス国債の対独利回り差は急拡大し、一時欧州債務危機（2012年）以来の水準に。その後、大統領選の有力候補と
目されるルペン氏（国民連合）が財政赤字を縮小する意欲を示し、拡大はひとまず一服
―ECBによる介入（国債買い入れ等）の可能性については、複数の高官が検討段階にないとの見方を示す
 長期金利の上昇は需要を抑制。ECB高官からは追加利上げの必要性が低下するとの見方も
― エネルギー高によるインフレ圧力と金利上昇による景気下押し圧力が併存。ECBの政策判断は一段と難しくなる可能性

中国：7～9月期の実質GDPは＋4.5％。インフラ投資の加速で、通年の成長率目標を確保へ
 7～9月期の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋4.5％。1～9月の累計で同＋4.6％と、通年目標「＋4.5～5.0％」の範囲内
―7～9月期は、純輸出が＋1.8％Ptと成長をけん引する一方、総資本形成は＋0.6％Ptまで縮小。内需の弱さ鮮明
― 中国政府は地方政府専項債の発行枠を追加（2,500億元）。インフラ投資を加速させ、成長率目標の達成を確保へ
 9月の小売は同＋1.1％と、前月（同＋0.4％）から加速。メモリー高騰が通信機器の売上額（同＋31.1％）を押し上げ
― 投資は同▲7.2％（前月：同▲10.6％） 。前年の反動増は限定的で、インフラ投資は同▲10.5％と2ケタ減が続く

― 生産は同＋5.5％（前月：同＋5.2％）。生産量では、産業用ロボット（同＋18.9％）や集積回路（同＋18.4％）が堅調

中国：政府は住宅ローンの利子補給で市場テコ入れ。人民元相場は緩やかな元高が継続
 中国政府が9/28、新規住宅ローン金利の年率1％分を補助する政策を発表。低迷が続く不動産市場をテコ入れ
― 対象物件の購入金額を150万元（3,500万円）以下に限定し、低中所得者層の住宅ローン負担を軽減する
― これにより、余剰在庫に苦しむ地方都市での実需を掘り起こし、市況のさらなる悪化に歯止めをかけたい考え
 人民元相場は、中国の貿易黒字が過去最高水準を記録する中、緩やかに元高が進行し1ドル＝6.6元台に突入
― 高水準の貿易黒字を背景とした人民元買いの実需が強いことから、当面は緩やかな元高が続く見込み
― ただ、米FRBの利上げで米中金利差が拡大しており、さらなる拡大が意識されれば元安に転じる可能性も

日本：旺盛なAI関連需要が製造業の景況感を押し上げ。利益計画は大幅上方修正
16
 大企業の業況判断は製造業が6四半期連続の改善。非製造業は悪化も一時的な悪天候が主因で変調の懸念は低い
― 業種別では、化学、石油・石炭製品、生産用機械等が改善。旺盛なAI関連需要や原油の供給不足懸念の後退等が背景

― とりわけ大企業・製造業では、輸出向け売上高の上方修正が顕著。上期の実績が大幅に上振れたことを反映したもの
― 世界的なAI関連需要の好調持続が見込まれるもと、下期も堅調な輸出が続けば、利益計画は一段の上方修正余地

― 積み上がった受注残を段階的に消化することで、設備投資が持続的に下支えされる公算が大きい

日本：コメ価格下落で企業物価は鈍化。もっとも、先行きは原油高を背景に再加速へ
18
 9月の国内企業物価はコメ価格の下落を主因に、前年比＋6.8％（前月：同＋7.6％）と鈍化
― 新米価格が昨年を下回ったこと等を背景に、コメ価格が大幅に下落。国内企業物価を▲0.75%Pt押し下げ
― 一方、3月来の原油高を背景に化学・プラスチック製品は高止まり。また、AI需要等を背景に非鉄金属も高い伸びを維持
 7月以降の原油高再燃を背景に、先行きの国内企業物価は再加速する見込み
― 原油価格高止まりにタンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの輸入物価は原油関連を中心に加速へ
― 国内企業物価は石油・石炭製品やラグを伴いつつ化学・プラスチック製品などを中心に再加速する見込み
（注） コメは玄米及び精米。食品関連は、飲食料品及び農林水産物（除くコメ）

米国債券市場：インフレ加速懸念で上昇後、AI関連株の下落を受け金利は低下
 10月の米10年国債利回りは、一時5.3％台まで上昇したものの、足元では5.0％前後まで低下
― 月初は、原油高等によるインフレ加速懸念を受け、長期金利は約24年ぶりの高水準となる5.3％台まで上昇
― 中旬以降は、AI関連株の先行きに対する慎重な見方から、安全資産である米国債への需要が拡大し、長期金利は低下
 今後も、中東情勢に伴う原油価格の変動やAI関連株の動向など、金利を左右する不確実性が多く、振れの大きい展開が続
く可能性

国内債券・為替：株安を背景に長期金利は低下。ドル円は150円台後半で横ばい圏
 長期金利は、株安を背景に低下。10年金利・30年金利とも、10月上旬から30bps以上の大幅低下
― 米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格急落が株安の契機となり、安全資産とされる日本国債に資金が流入
― 先行き、株式市場の混乱が続けば、短期的には一段の金利低下余地も。もっとも、日銀は四半期に一度のペースで利上
げを続けるとみられ、混乱一巡後の円金利は、再び緩やかな上昇基調に転じる見通し
 ドル円は150円台後半で方向感に欠ける展開。先行きも、150円台を中心とした円安水準が続く見込み
― 原油高は円安要因の一方、株安に伴うリスクオフや仏情勢悪化を受けたユーロ安は円高要因。目先は両者が綱引きへ

内外株式：AIインフラ投資への信用不安の高まりから、AI・半導体関連株を中心に下落

― 企業業績への期待から、10月初の日米株は上昇。S&P500は一時最高値を更新。日経平均も一時7万円台を回復
― その後は、米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格の急落を受け、AI・半導体関連株を中心に下落。原油価格の上昇に伴

うインフレ懸念も下押し圧力に。もっとも、S&P500はヘルスケア関連株等への資金シフトが進み、底堅く推移
 AIインフラ投資に対する信用不安は残存。原油価格の上昇も重石となり、日米株は軟調な推移が続くと予想
― 金利が高水準で推移する中、AIインフラの過剰投資懸念が強まり株価を下押しするリスクには留意が必要

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期 ，維持政策利率不變。 決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂 ， 也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席Kevin Warsh在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在 「通膨高企」 與 「經濟強韌」 雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下 ，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變 。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息 ， 為歐元

提供部分支撐 ， 但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國 ， 在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下 ， 歐元反彈力道相對有限 。 目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善 ， 但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣持續走升。周初市場等待美國聯準會利率決議，交投偏向觀望，境
外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪； 周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變 ，美元指數自 102
上方回落 ， 加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐， 境外人民幣突破6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升 。 就中國國內市場來看 ，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個
月新高，預計將持續帶動人民幣走升；就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持
利率不變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，顯示聯準會對通膨仍保持警戒，市場預期
美國利率將維持高檔 ， 美中利差維持在相對高位 ，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度 ；
最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散 ，匯價維持在五日均
線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣
偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6200-6.6700

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 上漲。週初，美伊衝突仍陷僵局支撐美元避險買盤，USD/JPY 最
高來到 158.34；週間，聯準會公布利率維持利率不變，USD/JPY 持續在 158 附近盤
整；臨近週末，日本央行公布利率維持不變並釋放偏鷹訊號， USD/JPY 最低來到
156.94，終場收在 157.53，週線上漲 72 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：156.00-158.50

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息 ， 升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢 。二 、 歐元區第三季GDP
年增 1.3%， 高於市場預期的1.1%， 顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下 ，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場 。 此外 ，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%， 優於市場預期的3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下 ， 市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩 ，預計將限縮歐元走升空間 。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破 ， 顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上 ， 預估EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

We hosted EMC’s 3Q26 post result call on October 29th. Key 
takeaways include: 
(1) EMC mentioned its proactive capacity expansion plans in coming 
years (capacity will go up to 15.25mn sheets/month by end of 2030 
vs. 5.85mn sheets/month now) are still not enough to support all the 
LTA they signed for the next 4 years, implying a solid demand 
outlook without much risk on oversupply.  
(2) In terms of the CCL pricing, the company mentioned it’s doing 
the second round of the high-end CCL (M7+) pricing hike this year, 
with 5-20% pricing hike ratio, which not only on M7/M8, but also on 
M8+/M9 grade CCL.  
(3) For 4Q26, EMC mentioned the conservative estimate on revenue 
growth rate should be at least 15-20% QoQ, with GM could at least 
expand to 38.0%-39.5% (note that the company estimated 3Q rev 
could grow 15% QoQ and GM could be 33.5%-36.5% back to 2Q26 
result call - here, but the actual outcome was revenue up 29% QoQ 
and GM hit 37.7%).  
(4) For substrate material, the company mentioned it is focusing on 
the high-end ABF substrate core-layer CCL (BT type, but for ABF 
substrate), and will hold 50% market share for the MTK TPU v8 ABF 
substrate. EMC also mentioned that it is now working with several 
customers on high end ABF used CCL qualification projects which 
the company expects to start shipment from 4Q27. 
(5) For the substrate material capacity, EMC now holds 300k 
sheets/month capacity, but will expand the capacity to at least 
1.2mn sheets/month by end of 2027. EMC mentioned the proactive 
expansion is their strategy on always prepare capacity ahead of 
customers’ demand, and mentioned these new capacity can also be 
used to produce high-end M9/M10/M11 CCL if the demand cannot 
fill all the capacity.

### Page 3

(6) In terms of the profitability of the substrate material, the company mentioned the
GM of this product will be at least 50%+, despite its relatively competitive pricing
compares to the market leader who has 65%+ GM on substrate material.
(7) EMC mentioned it does see AI customers start qualifying second or third sources to
improve the stability of the supply chain, which should give more room for competitors
to enter the market. However, the company continues to target 50% AI CCL market

share in the long term, and believes its best in class product quality and deep
cooperation with PCB/CSP customers can see it continue to hold the leadership position
in this high-end industry.
Investment view - read across for high-end CCL players
We maintain our positive view on the high-end CCL players, including EMC and TUC,
while we see both companies are enjoying not only favorable pricing conditions on
high-end CCL (due to the wide supply demand gap) but also the accelerating product

mix improvement speed (we expect M9 massive adoption to start from 2H27 with 50%+
adoption rate for next generation AI server, and expect EMC to start M10 CCL product
shipment from 2H27 with mass volume shipment to start from 2028). See our Oct 28
report.
Both EMC and TUC mentioned the proactive capacity expansion they are planning in the
next 3-5 years (both companies will have 150-250% more capacity in 2029-2030 vs.
today) are still not enough to support the high-end AI/LEO/switching customers’

demand, suggesting an even more favorable pricing outlook. We expect the high-end
CCL pricing will go up by 40%+ YoY in 2027 considering the increasing tightness
condition of the high-end CCL industry (vs. 20-30% YoY 2026), which should drive both
company’s GM further in coming years.
In terms of competition, while we already model EMC’s AI CCL market share to decease
from 60-70% in 2026 to ~50% in the next three years, we do believe EMC’s earnings will

grow by 90% 2026-29 CAGR even with this 50% market share. For TUC, we expect it
should be the key share gainer from EMC considering CSPs duo source policy (we think
TUC would be a strong option due to not only its better technology skillset but also its
proactive expansion plans while other high-end CCL players are not expanding capacity
or only expanding slightly on the capacity), and we expect the company will have
multiple AI customers including AI ASIC/GPU end customers from 2H27.

Overall, we are even more positive on EMC/TUC compared to 3 months ago, after seeing
customers solid plans on high-end CCL LTA and customers’ design upgrade on CCL spec
(we had expected M10 MP would happen in 2029 or beyond only). We believe
EMC/TUC’s key competition advantage is not only from the leadership position in the
high-end CCL market, but also their proactive capacity expansion plans which makes
them attractive to AI customers as most of the other high-end CCL players are not

expanding enough capacity in 2027/28.
Maintain Buy on EMC / TUC with TP of NT$11,700 / NT$3,460, implying 134%/150%
upside vs. last close.

### Page 5

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$11,700 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

營收走勢圖

研究員/游周翰 Jeffery.Yu@uni-psg.com

研究部維持聯亞買進投資建議，主要考量：(1)3Q26 營收及獲利皆
優於市場預期；(2)AI Data Center 矽光需求持續強勁，主要客戶
訂單能見度超過 6 個月，4Q26 營收可望續增，1Q27 新增產能開出
將帶動營收明顯跳增；(3)管理層維持先前 2027 年產能擴增指引
(+150%YoY)，並且確立2028 年產能擴增速度與 2027 年相當，亦即
兩年擴增產能超過 6 倍。惟考量(1)2026 年新增後段產能驗證時間
較研究部預期長；(2)3Q26 毛利率及費用率優於預期；(3)2027 年
產能增長幅度優於研究部預期 ， 因此調整2026/2027 年營收分別為
55.2 億元(原估 55.92 億)及 155.6 億元(原估 150.4 億)，EPS 分別
為 20.95 元(原估 19.96 元)及 58.76 元(原估 54.06 元)。目標從
3,800 上修至 4,000 元，重申買進投資建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26營收、EPS均優於市場預期，矽光出貨帶動獲利成長： 
聯亞3Q26營收15.62億元(+27.9%QoQ，+182.6%YoY)，略低研究
部預期，主因為後段產能驗證時間較研究部預期長 ； 營收雙增
主要還是受惠AI Data Center 矽光產品出貨量持續增加。毛
利率58.6%(+1.1pts QoQ，+15 pts YoY)，營業利益為7.71億
元，營益率達49.4%；營業費用1.43億元(+11%QoQ，+4%YoY)，
主因為研發及員工分紅費用增加；稅後淨利 6.21億元，EPS 
6.12元， 優於研究部預期 ， 主因為毛利率及費用控管優於研究
部預估。產品組合方面，Data Center占營收80–85%，其中以
矽光產品為主 ；Telecom占5–10%， 其他約5%， 與上季比重相當。

表一、3Q26 研究部預估與實際差異 
單位：百萬元 實際 研究部原估 差異 
營業收入 1,562 1,596 -2.1% 
營業利益 771 713 +8.1% 
稅後淨利 621 575 +8.0% 
EPS(元) 6.12 5.65 +8.0% 
資料來源： 聯亞財報、統一研究部預估

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 4Q26營收持續季增，1Q27新增產能帶動出貨加速： 
展望4Q26，主要客戶目前訂單期間已超過6個月，管理層表示
在手訂單金額已超過2026年全年營收規模， 顯現出目前需求
仍 然 十 分 強 勁 。 管 理 層 預 期 營 收 季 增幅 約 與4Q25 相當
(+16.2%QoQ)，即18.15億元 。 然而 ， 依照過往管理層給予的指
引通常較為保守，且在需求強勁以及新加入的 合作夥伴陸續
開出 後 段 產 能 下 ， 研 究 部 預 估 4Q26 營收為 18.32 億元
(+17.3%QoQ，+185.2%YoY)，EPS為6.77元。

展望1Q27，隨相關設備導入及後段委外產能開始量產，管理層
預期1Q27產出量將有更明顯增幅 。研究部預估 1Q27營收為
275.2億元(+50.2%QoQ，+204%YoY)，EPS為10.26。

3. 美國客戶四年約加持，2028年產出目標為2026年的6.25倍： 
聯亞8M26與美系Data Center 客戶簽訂2027–2030年為期四年
的CW Laser供貨長約 ， 客戶並以保證金方式提供資金支持 ， 以
確保未來產品供應 。 為履行長期供貨承諾 ， 公司同步追加近30
億元資本支出計畫，使過去一年累計核准資本支出超過 80億
元 ， 預計2027年產出顆數較2026年增加150%，2028年再較2027
年增加150%， 相當於2028年產出規模達2026年的6.25倍 ， 其中
8月新增資本支出主要貢獻於2028年。此外，為確保擴產後的
原料供應，公司亦與兩家日系及一家美系InP基板供應商簽訂
長期採購合約 ， 並持續增加台灣及海外後段委外代工夥伴 。 研
究部認為，美系客戶透過長期供貨合約及保證金支持聯亞擴
產 ， 顯示客戶對未來CW Laser需求具有高度確定性 ， 亦有助提
高新增產能的去化能見度，支撐公司2027–2028年營運成長。

4. 多波長CW Laser具技術及ASP優勢，NPO較CPO先行： 
聯亞800G及1.6T矽光產品已進入量產，分別具備8顆雷射及8
至16顆不同波長雷射的解決方案。管理層指出，多波長產品
ASP較單波長高出50%以上，且全球具備大規模量產能力的供
應商有限 ， 有利公司維持產品競爭優勢 。 隨未來光通訊傳輸規
格持續升級，公司亦積極開發3.2T及NPO/CPO應用所需的高功
率CW Laser。其中，管理層預期NPO有望於2H27率先作為過渡
方案，目前所接觸客戶以外部光源設計為主；CPO則因光源配
置 、 連接器等相關規格尚未完全定案 ， 大規模導入時程仍有待
觀察。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

此外，隨NPO/CPO等應用對光源功率要求提高，未來單顆 CW 
Laser功率提升至250–300mW以上 。 管理層指出 ， 高功率產品因
晶粒面積增加及良率下降，相同InP基板產能可產出的晶粒顆
數可能減少30–50%，使高功率光源對基板及有效產能的需求
增加 。 研究部認為 ， 短期聯亞仍以既有CW Laser產品擴產及多
波長方案出貨成長為主要動能，中長期則隨 NPO/CPO逐步導
入 ， 高功率光源有望成為新增需求來源 ， 並提高相關產品的製
造及供應門檻，將有望成為其技術護城河，維持競爭優勢。

5. Data Center高速PD完成驗證，預計隨新增產能逐步量產： 
除CW Laser外，聯亞亦持續布局Data Center高速PD產品。管
理層指出 ，200G以上PD已完成客戶驗證 ， 預計2027年新增產能
陸續開出，開始量產 Data Center PD 產品。惟目前仍以 CW 
Laser為主要生產重心，因此PD產出規模相對有限，但有助於
降低產品集中度風險。

表二、研究部預估調整

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

## Document 212: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) STRESS TEST삼성전자(005930) STRESS TESTSTRESS TEST - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) STRESS TEST삼성전자(005930) STRESS TESTSTRESS TEST - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
78 
삼성전자  (005930) 
2026.11.04

STRESS TEST 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

목표주가 37만원, ‘28년 기준으로는 56만원까지 열림 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 37만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27 년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3 배를 적용했다. 이는 ‘00년 이후 25년간 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 
해당한다[표1]. ‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 56%, 5 9%, 4 8%로 지난 25년 평균인 
22%를 크게 상회할 전망이며, 고원형 ROE가 장기간 유지될 것으로 판단한다.  
이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해질 경우 시장의 시야는 ‘28년으로 향할 것이다. 
이 경우 ‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 56만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 43% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -41%를 넘어섰다. 특히 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
이러한 우려가 지속되는 한, 이익의 눈높이가 높아질수록 멀티플은 저렴해질 
뿐이며, DRAM 가격이 예상보다 더 올라갈 것이라는 소식이 주가의 촉매로 
작용하지 못한다. 결국 주가 반등을 위해서는 시장이 상정하고 있는 다운사이클이 
우려만큼 깊지 않을 것이라는 믿음이 필요한 것으로 본다.

STRESS TEST와 HBM 재평가 
① 당사는 고객들이 어느 정도 가격대에서 LTA를 해지하려는 유인이 생길지 
추정해봤다. Micron의 코멘트를 차용하여 ‘22~’23년 수준의 스트레스 테스트를 
시나리오 분석한 결과, 4Q27 고점 대비 DRAM 가격이 60% 수준 빠져야 해지가 
검토될 수 있다[그림2]. 그마저도 다음 공급 부족기의 물량 배정 우선순위 상실 
등 정성적 요인까지 감안하면 실제 해지 가능성은 더욱 낮은 것으로 판단한다. 
② HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 
중장기적으로 확대될 cHBM은 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 
재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 물량과 가격이 결정된다. 수요가 둔화되더라도 범용 
메모리처럼 재고 누적과 가격 급락으로 이어지기보다는 발주 조정이나 계약 
재협상을 통해 영향을 받는 구조다.  
동사 HBM 매출은 ‘26년 28조원에서 ‘28년 215조원으로 증가할 전망이다. 
‘27년 HBM 가격 방향성의 윤곽이 잡힌 현 시점에서, 동사 HBM 매출의 변수는 
가격이 아니라 물량이며, 물량은 고객과 사전에 협의되는 구조다. 과거 메모리 
업황에서는 2년 뒤의 이익을 가늠하기 어려웠으나, 이제는 HBM을 통해 중장기 
실적을 논의할 수 있게 됐다는 점, 그 자체에 주목한다. 
만약 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E (21배, 15배, 12배)를 
적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 19%, 69%, 108%를 설명한다[표3]. 
특히 cHBM이 본격화할 ‘28년에는 HBM만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명될 수 
있다는 의미다. 시장이 통상 받아들이는 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 다르지 
않다.

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간일 전망이다.  
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 업황 지속성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 이 불확실성이 
멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 
구간이 이어질 전망이다. 기회비용은 결국 환원수익률로 보상받아야 하므로, ‘27년 1월 신규 
주주환원 정책이 중요한 변곡점이 될 것으로 판단한다.  
당사는 동사가 ‘27~’29년 FCF의 50%를 주주에게 환원하고, 이 중 70%를 배당, 30%를 
자사주 매입/소각으로 쓰는 것으로 가정하여, 현재 주가 기준 ‘27년 15% 수준의 
주주환원수익률을 기대한다. 미국 10년물 국채 금리가 5% 안팎인 환경에서 무위험 금리의 
3배에 달하는 환원수익률, 그중 배당만으로 10% 수준의 수익률은 그 자체로 희소한 투자 
포인트로 볼 수 있다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는, 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 이후 신규 팹 물량이 
소량 출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 LTA가 이익을 지킨다는 사실이 
숫자로 확인되고 cHBM의 가치가 재평가된다면 순환 할인이 축소될 것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

## Document 216: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.  
목표주가 (유지) 460,000원  
현재가 (10/1) 276,000원

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 243: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면  이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 263: AI编程之后下一个爆发点需等待---摩根大通双双下调智谱和MiniMax

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI编程之后下一个爆发点需等待---摩根大通双双下调智谱和MiniMax.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

摩根⼤通下调智谱⽬标价⾄ 1200 港元、维持增持， MiniMax 下调⾄ 200 港元、维持中性，
估值倍数由 20 倍压缩⾄ 12 倍 2030 年预期市盈率，主要反映下⼀波企业 AI 需求拐点晚于预
期。但摩根⼤通认为市场反应过度，当前市场处于编码与更⼴泛⽩领⼯作需求之间的 " 空档
期 " ，下⼀个需求台阶是时间问题。
摘要：
摩根⼤通下调智谱和 MiniMax ⽬标价，认为AI 编码需求之后的下⼀波企业需求拐点到来时间晚于
预期，但判断市场反应过度，仍维持对中国 AI 模型⾏业的整体看涨⽴场。
11 ⽉ 3 ⽇，摩根⼤通徐⽂韬团队将智谱⽬标价从 2,000 港元下调⾄ 1,200 港元，评级维持 " 增持 " ；
将 MiniMax ⽬标价从 300 港元下调⾄ 200 港元，评级维持 " 中性 " 。
两只股票的估值基准均由 20 倍 2030 年预期市盈率下调⾄ 12 倍，主要反映下⼀个需求场景拐点时
间的不确定性，不过智谱仍是摩根⼤通的⾸选股。
两只股票均已从 2026 年峰值⼤幅回调，智谱跌约 78% 、 MiniMax 跌约 85% 。

但摩根⼤通认为，token 使⽤量持续攀升、算⼒约束⽽⾮需求疲软制约中国市场、以及智谱⽑利
率持续改善等多重信号表明，当前市场对⾏业的悲观推断存在偏差。
编码之后的 " 空档期 " ：需求分步迈进
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

摩根⼤通将 AI 模型企业市场类⽐为劳动⼒市场：模型是劳动⼒，企业是雇主，模型公司则是兼营
⼈事业务的培训学校。
在这⼀框架下，企业对 AI 模型的需求是以 " 台阶式 " 跳升，只有当模型能够可靠完成⼀类新⼯作
时，企业才会⼤规模 " 录⽤ " ，此后任务量可能急剧增加。
据摩根⼤通，⽬前市场正处于编码与更⼴泛⽩领⼯作两个台阶之间的空档期。编码需求已验证
这⼀模式。
Cursor 年化收⼊从 2025 年 11 ⽉约 10 亿美元增⾄ 2026 年 6 ⽉逾 40 亿美元（据 Dealroom ），触发点
正是编码智能体变得可靠的时间节点。法律科技公司 Harvey 的 ARR 在不到两年内增⻓约 8 倍，显
⽰同样的路径正在其他垂直⾏业复制。
摩根⼤通指出，今天其他⽩领⼯作的状态，类似于 2024 年的编码⼯作，需求初露端倪但尚未爆
发。
独⽴测评机构 METR 的数据显⽰，当前最优模型在约 80% 的情况下能完成 3 ⼩时⼯作量的任务，
⽽模型可独⽴完成的任务时⻓⼤约每 105 天翻⼀番。摩根⼤通据此判断，下⼀个需求台阶 " 是时

间问题 " 。

### Page 3

与此同时，摩根⼤通强调算⼒供应约束是压制中国 AI 收⼊的主要因素。
商品化与定价权并存，价格下跌不等于利润率受损
市场另⼀⼤担忧是 token 价格持续下跌是否代表实验室丧失定价权。摩根⼤通认为两者可以并
存，并以智谱和美国市场数据加以佐证。
据 Ramp AI 指数，⾃ 2025 年 8 ⽉以来，美国样本中最⼤供应商的每百万 token 综合价格已减半，
但企业 token ⽀出增⾄约 15 倍，原因是 token 消耗量增⾄约 36 倍。
中国⽅⾯， 2024 年价格战令 API 标价下跌 90% 以上，此后⽇均 token 调⽤量增加逾千倍，从 2024
年初约 1,000 亿增⾄ 2026 年 6 ⽉超过 500 万亿。
摩根⼤通将模型分层定价的逻辑与学历市场类⽐：⽼⼀代模型等同于低学历，遭遇降薪（降价）
以换取使⽤量；⽽率先达到新能⼒标准的顶尖模型享有溢价窗⼝期。
过去两个⽉，中美七家头部实验室发布逾⼗款新模型，逾半数定价低于前代，但 OpenAI 的 GPT-
6 Astra 和⾕歌的 Gemini 4 Argon 两款顶尖新品均实现提价，摩根⼤通认为这印证了定价权依然

存在于前沿模型层⾯。

### Page 4

在⻓智能体任务中，质量的重要性尤为突出：单步准确率从 95% 提升⾄ 99% ， 20 步任务的成功
率可从 36% 跳升⾄ 82% ，使得顶尖模型的替代品⽆法仅凭低价取胜。智谱的 GLM-5.3 API 价格较
前代提⾼⼀倍，同时推出低价版 GLM-5.3-Flash 留住价格敏感⽤⼾，以 " 双杆并拉 " 的⽅式兼顾份
额与定价权。
智谱开放平台及 API 业务⽑利率在 2026 年上半年从⼀年前的 -0.4% 升⾄ 24.6% ，摩根⼤通认为随
着芯⽚、基础设施和模型硬件协同设计持续改善，⽑利率还有进⼀步提升空间。
智谱：前沿地位稳固， GLM-5.5/6 为近期催化剂
摩根⼤通将智谱定位为 " 中国少数仍在培养前沿毕业⽣的学校之⼀ " ，认为在需求价值持续向前沿
企业集聚的格局下，智谱具备持续捕获最⼤机会的条件。
智谱 ARR 增⻓轨迹印证了上述判断：从 2025 年 12 ⽉约 3,500 万美元增⾄ 2026 年 8 ⽉ 18 亿美元，约
8 个⽉内增⻓约 46 倍；管理层随后将年末指引从 24 亿美元上调⾄ 30 亿美元。公司 9 ⽉通过增发及

可转债筹集 50 亿美元，⽤于⽀持下⼀代模型训练和算⼒投⼊。
摩根⼤通将 2026 年收⼊预测从 61 亿元⼈⺠币下调⾄ 57 亿元⼈⺠币，同时将 2027 年收⼊预测从
178 亿元上调⾄ 239 亿元。⽬标价下调主要源于估值倍数压缩 ——新⽬标价基于 12 倍 2030 年预期
市盈率，剔除了此前给予公司相对于国内⼀线互联⽹企业的⼤部分溢价。
下⼀个近期观察点是预计 11 ⽉发布的 GLM-5.5/GLM-6 ，模型参数预计达 2.5 万亿⾄ 3.5 万亿。摩
根⼤通将关注新模型是否缩⼩与美国前沿模型的差距，以及企业是否开始将其⽤于更⻓的多步
骤智能体⼯作。
MiniMax ：性价⽐策略受认可，但前沿地位尚待证明
相较智谱，摩根⼤通对 MiniMax 态度审慎，评级维持 " 中性 " 。
MiniMax 今年主要在性价⽐层⾯竞争， M3 和 M3.1 Flash 均以领先实验室中最低区间的价格提供
有实⼒的模型，但公司全年未上调 ARR 指引，摩根⼤通认为这表明其性价⽐策略尚未转化为企

业需求的实质升级。
摩根⼤通同样将 2030 年预期市盈率倍数从 20 倍下调⾄ 12 倍，对应⽬标价从 300 港元下调⾄ 200 港
元。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

缩⼩与中美领先模型的差距，并按前沿模型定价，⽽⾮延续性价⽐路线。研报指出：
摩根⼤通列出的主要下⾏⻛险包括：追随者能⼒追赶速度加快导致溢价窗⼝期缩短、拥有云业

## Document 321: EcoNews of 26 October 2026 (2026-10-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/EcoNews of 26 October 2026 (2026-10-26).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.39 / 26 October 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
In brief. The week was dominated by a continued global selloff in tech
stocks, leading to safe haven flows into US Treasuries, Japanese Go -
vernment bonds and German bunds. As a result, their yields fell des -
pite energy prices surging to or past their Spring highs. Economic data
generally showed continued resilience. French bonds were under rising

pressure, but Moody's unexpected decision to maintain its France ra -
ting unchanged could, at the margin, help stabilise the spread. The Fed,
ECB and BOJ should be on hold next week while signaling further rate
action is likely.
EUROPEAN UNION
A win for securitization. The Council and the European Parliament have
reached a provisional political agreement on measures to revive and
simplify the EU’s securitization market. The updated framework will

reduce banks’ capital requirements for the safest and most well-struc-
tured transactions, notably for a newly introduced “resilient” category
of securitization. As a key component of the “One Europe, One Market”
initiative and the EU’s Savings and Investment Union agenda, this aims
to channel increased lending to households and businesses. The rele -
vant legal texts must now be finalised.
EUROZONE
Expansion continues. The flash PMIs weakened in October, mainly on

the back of a decline in France. The EZ composite index dipped to 51.9
(-1.2 m/m), dragged down by services (51.7, -1.3 m/m), although ma -
nufacturing also dipped (52.4, -0.5 m/m). Both remain in expansion
territory, and manufacturing new export orders rose to their highest
level since early 2022. Price signals were also rather reassuring, with
the services output price index easing to 54.9 (-1.7 m/m). Households
are more cautious: consumer confidence fell for a second consecutive

month to a five-month low (-17.9). Households’ inflation expectations
edged up at short horizons (+0.1 pp to 3.6% at one year and 3.1% at
three years), but long-term expectations remained anchored, with the
five-year median stable at 2.5%.
France
Rising market stress despite decent economic news; a reprieve from
Moody’s. The spread on French 10-year bond relative to Germany’s
peaked to 174 bps on Friday, the highest since 2011 and only 16 bps
short of its all-time high, as markets price in anticipation of worst-case

scenarios regarding the country’s ability to repair its public finances.
The 10Y yield briefly exceeded 5.1% before falling back to 5% (above
the US equivalent). Nonetheless, services output was stable in August,
following a 0.6% m/m increase in July, a much higher pace than in Q2.
The rebound is driven by the catering sector, the digital sector, business
services, and real estate. The INSEE business climate improved from
96 to 97 (long-term average = 100) in October, but the survey was

conducted before the riots. The main improvement was in manufactu-
ring, which rose from 101 to 105, driven by increased new orders in the
aeronautics sector, and to a lesser extent by the services sector (from
98 to 99). The PMI survey (conducted partially after the riots) was
less positive: the composite index fell to 48.4 (-2.7 m/m), with a sharp
decline in services (48.4; -2.8 m/m) and a slightly smaller decline in
manufacturing (49.3; -1.3 m/m). Moody's unexpectedly announced af -

ter the market close on Friday that it was maintaining France’s ra -
ting at Aa3 on account of France’s diversified economic structure and
sound public institutions, but maintained its negative outlook. This is
on par with the UK and one notch above S&P and Fitch. Moody’s made
clear keeping this rating is conditional on a budget being adopted that
reduces the fiscal deficit, along with an easing of social and market
stress going forward. Saturday’s demonstrations across the country

saw strong attendance but only limited violent incidents.
Germany
Prices continue to rise, but confidence surveys remain on an upward
trend. Producer prices rose 6.7% y/y in September (+2.1pp m/m; the
highest level in 3.5 years) due to higher prices for energy and inter -
mediate goods, primarily metals. The ZEW Economic Sentiment Index
improved beyond consensus forecasts in October and returned to its
highest level since May 2023 (-35.9; +11.2 m/m), albeit the Economic

Expectations Index fell to its lowest level since July (28.1; -6.6 m/m).
The recovery in economic activity continues, although a slowdown is
evident: the flash Composite PMI slowed (52.8; -1 m/m; the lowest in
2 months) due to weaker growth in the services sector (51.7; -1.1 m/m;
the lowest in 2 months) and in manufacturing output (55.1; -0.9 m/m;
the lowest in 3 months). Nevertheless, in both sectors, hiring is rising
slightly, supported by growth in export sales and the spillover effects of

investment plans. Despite persistently high input prices, selling prices
are slowing (reaching their lowest level since February in the manu -
facturing sector).
UNITED KINGDOM
Inflation, wage growth and activity accelerate. CPI inflation overshot
consensus on energy prices, printing at 0.4% m/m and 3.5% y/y in Sep-
tember (consensus: 3.4%, prior: 3.1%), its highest in 11 months. Core
CPI came in at 2.8% y/y (up 0.2 pp), in line with consensus. Pressures

remained persistent according to the PPI at 8.3% y/y (prior: 6.7%). La -
bour market and activity data also surprised positively. Payroll growth
reached 102k in August (3m/3m), bringing the unemployment rate to
4.7% (down 0.2 pp), its lowest since July 2025. October PMI surveys
beat expectations in both manufacturing (52.8, consensus: 51.8, prior:
51.9) and services (53.1, consensus: 51.8, prior: 52.1). The only negative
news came from headline retail sales, falling 0.4% m/m in September,

with the core gauge (excluding petrol) deteriorating by -0.7% m/m.
UNITED STATES
Construction struggling, GENIUS Act implemented. Housing starts
dropped -5.2% m/m in September (-7.4% y/y). This third consecutive
monthly decline dragged the Q3 average to its lowest since Q2 2020.
Building permits also came in negative, at -0.2% m/m (-3.0% y/y), while
the NAHB homebuilder sentiment index plunged to its weakest since
2012. The average 30-year fixed mortgage rate eased to 7.32% (still

134bp above pre-war level), as the ongoing AI selloff pushed down
US treasury yields. The GENIUS Act final implementation rule from the
Office of the Comptroller of the Currency’s (OCC) leaves the door open
for stablecoin customers to receive yield or rewards. Issuers remain
prohibited from paying holders any form of interest or yield solely
in connection with the holding, use, or retention of payment stable -
coins. This represents a shift from the OCC’s February proposal and

has renewed concerns within the banking industry about deposit flight.
The rule will take effect on January 18, 2027.
JAPAN
Activity and inflation keep rising. Composite PMI was unchanged in
September at 52.3, solidly in expansion territory, as an improvement
in services activity offset slower output growth in manufacturing. The
Manufacturing PMI disappointed at 53.3 (-0.8 m/m; an 8-month low).
Underlying price pressures, meanwhile, extended. Headline CPI was

7

flat on a monthly basis, bringing the annual rate up to 2.1% (up 0.4 pp).
Core CPI (excl. fresh food) matched it at 2.1% y/y (up 0.4 pp), in line
with consensus. More importantly, the New Core index, which strips
out fresh food and energy, rose sharply to 2.4% y/y (up 0.5 pp), its
fastest pace since January.
EMERGING ECONOMIES

ASIA
China
New monetary policy tool and export controls. The central bank’s Go-
vernor Pan Gongsheng unveiled a plan to switch (“when appropriate”)
its key policy rate from the 7-day reverse repo rate (at 1.4% since May
2025) to the overnight reverse repo rate, in order to reflect develop -
ments in the interbank market. New export controls: The government
plans to add 40 key technologies to the list of technologies prohibited
or restricted for export, including in the AI (for ex. LLMs, computing

clusters), biotechnology and photovoltaic sectors.
Indonesia
BI kept policy rate unchanged at 5.75%, raised its deposit facility rate
by 25bp to 5% and announced a phased cut in reserve requirements
from 9% to 7% by December. This combination of limited FX support
and measures to support credit growth sends mixed signals amidst
pressures on the rupiah following MSCI’s downgrade warning and un-
dermines the central bank’s credibility. The rupiah has lost 1% against

the USD since mid-October, with little contagion effect on other Asian
currencies.
South Korea
The Bank of Korea (BoK) kept its policy rate unchanged at 3.0%, but
the tightening cycle may not be over. CPI inflation was 2.9% y/y in Sep-
tember, above the central bank’s target of 2%, while high oil prices,
rapid growth in Seoul housing prices and wage pressures (though
concentrated in the electronics sector) raise the risk of lasting infla -
tion pressure. As economic activity remains robust, supported by strong

exports and recovering investment, the BoK has scope to raise rates
further.
EMEA
Hungary
Monetary status quo and 2027 budget in consolidation mode. The
Central Bank kept its policy rate unchanged at 5.5%, as expected. A cau-
tious stance will likely prevail in the short term. Inflation has been
picking up in recent months, albeit mildly, and will continue to move
upward as the government recently removed the margin cap on fuel.
Separately, the Budget for 2027 and the medium-term fiscal plan show

Hungary’s goal to join the Eurozone; the government expects to reduce
the fiscal deficit to 4.9% of GDP in 2027 (from 7% expected in 2026) and
towards 2.8% of GDP by 2030.
Türkiye
Cautious monetary loosening. The Central Bank lowered its policy rate
by 100 bp to 36% at its October Monetary Policy Committee meeting,
in line with market expectations. The overnight lending and borrowing
rates were also reduced by 100 bp, to 39% and 34.5%, respectively.

The improvement in underlying inflation has yet to translate into a
sustained period of low inflation, justifying the CBRT’s preference for a
measured pace of monetary easing.
LATIN AMERICA
Brazil
President Lula da Silva was re-elected on Sunday for a fourth term
(with 51.8% of the vote), defeating far right-wing candidate Flavio Bol-
sonaro (48.2%). Lula will enter this new term facing significant econo -
mic and political challenges. Public debt has risen to more than 82% of

GDP , while high borrowing costs and rigid mandatory spending leave
limited room to reconcile fiscal consolidation with social spending and
investment. Restoring confidence in the fiscal outlook will be a key
challenge in the short term.
COMMODITIES
Pressure rising. Amid growing rumours of imminent re-escalation of
military operations in the Gulf, energy prices rose sharply this week,
with brent touching $117/b – near its April peak, and European gas

prices (TTF) rising to 97 €/MWh on Monday, their highest level since
Dec 2022. The Asian gas benchmark (JKM) is following the same trend
(4.6% w/w to 31.4 $/mbtu, equivalent to around 96 €/MWh). Diesel
crack spreads are slightly decreasing in Europe (-2.8% w/w on Monday)
and in the US (-4% w/w on Monday). The first Russian diesel tanker
intended to the US market left Russia this Friday and should supply the
US market in 2 weeks. US diesel average retail prices remain oriented

to the upside (0.7% w/w at 6.32 $/gallon on Monday). US commercial
crude inventories fell by 3.1 mb in the week ending 16 October, signi -
ficantly exceeding market expectations for a 340,000-barrel decline.

出，費城半導體指數漲勢尤其明顯 。雖然近

► 隨人工智慧運算需求持續攀升 ，谷歌、亞馬
遜、臉書、微軟及OpenAI等企業巨頭積極發
展客製化晶片 (ASIC)，藉此提升運算效率並
優化成 本 。根據 凱 基投顧 引述產 業數 據預
估，2027年客製化晶片出貨量將年增 92%至
1,446萬顆 ，首度超 越繪圖處理器 (GPU)的
1,129萬顆 ，顯示AI晶片市場正由 GPU主導
逐步轉向多元化發展。
► 在客製化晶片方面 ，谷歌持續擴大自研張量
處理器 (TPU)的應用與對外供應 ，亞馬遜亦
積極拓展自研AI晶片(Trainium)的雲端運算服
務，預估兩者合計將佔 2027年客製化晶片出
貨量約80%。
► 隨客製 化晶 片需 求 快速增 加 ，相 關晶 片設
計、先 進製 程及 先 進封裝 需求可 望同 步提
升，涉及上述業務發展的台股供應鏈 (註)有機
會受到市場關注。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 2024年全球工廠運作的工業機器人約 470萬
台，為10年前的 3倍，主要為韓國 、中國和
日本等製造業大國將其運用於汽車 、電子和
金屬加工等產業 ，並因而維持成本和品質優
勢，為競爭力重要來源之一 。雖然美國工業
機器人的普及程度仍低於亞洲主要製造業國
家，但近年積極尋求重建工業產能 、將關鍵
供應鏈遷回美國國內以及擴大半導體和製藥
生產，有可能讓美國逐步調整工業和製造層
面的策略方向。
► 傳統工業機器人與人形機器人在外觀設計與
應用場景上有明顯差異 。工業機器人通常部
署於高度標準化的環境 ，以執行範圍相對有
限且重複性高的特定作業為主 ，而人形機器
人則被設計用於多變的環境 ，並經常需要與
人類協同工作 ，因此需結合移動能力 、感知
能力與靈巧操作能力 ，才能夠執行多樣化任
務，而非受限單一功能。
工業機器人技術相對成熟且部署速度較快全球運作中的工業機器人數量穩定成長
工業機器人技術成熟且穩定成長，為製造業強國的重要競爭力來源

### Page 7

投資焦點人形機器人
► 人 形機 器 人 主 要由 結 合半 導 體 與AI 模 型的
「大腦」，並由感測器 、執行器 、線材、連
接器及輕量化電池驅動的機身所組成 ，可透
過感知環境資訊 、思考並進行協調的實體動
作。投行預估至2030年，執行器將佔整體人
形機器人成本超過 50%，為價值含量最高的
核心零組件 ，麥肯錫公司甚至認為執行器與
感測器可能成為未來供應鏈中的關鍵瓶頸。
► 機器人在實體世界中執行任務時 ，任何延遲
或連線中斷都可能帶來重大風險 ，因此相較
純數位的 AI應用 ，對即時運算 (邊緣運算 )能
力的要求更高 。而近幾年邊緣運算軟硬體能
力大幅提升 ，使本地端設備得以部署數十億
參數的 AI模型 ，並將反應延遲縮短至約 0.3
秒，使機器人能夠即時感知 、決策與行動 。
此外，過去6年風險資本對機器人、感測器及
IT 硬體的每年平均投資約 100 億美元 ， 但
2026年僅上半年即已達 280億美元 ，暗示市
場對機器人產業潛力的期待有別於過往。

### Page 8

8
股市
◆ 美、歐、日央行升息抑通膨 ，公債殖利率走高壓抑股債市場近期動能 。然而包括油價自高檔回落使
通膨壓力稍緩 ，以及美股第三季獲利年增率仍為雙位數成長 ，股市表現有支撐 。市場持續觀察AI資
本支出是否帶來獲利成長 ，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場動盪 ，可分散配置於不同國家與產業 ，降
低市場波動影響 。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評價面壓力等紛擾帶來負面影響 ，可布局防
禦股抗震，長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
◆ 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
◆ 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示 ，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間 ，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展 ，加上9月製造業及服務業PMI優於預期，殖利率偏高環境建議

以中短天期債券投資為主 ，可逢利率走升時布局進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流
與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
◆ 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
◆ 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
◆ 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
◆ 中東地緣衝突加劇推升油價，短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
◆ 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法

### Page 15

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 366: Kicking and screaming_ Child care costs persist Child care remains a constraint for many families’ budgets, with the average share of monthly child care 8% of a household’s pay. 2 

Source PDF: all report pdf/Kicking and screaming_ Child care costs persist Child care remains a constraint for many families’ budgets, with the average share of monthly child care 8% of a household’s pay. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13030323     1

Economy 
Kicking and screaming: Child care costs persist 
20 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Child care remains a growing financial burden for many households, even as growth in the median payment has moderated, 
according to Bank of America payments data. More families are paying more than $2,000 per month for care, suggesting 
affordability pressures are becoming increasingly concentrated among households facing the highest expenses. 
• Child care cost pressures vary significantly across the country. Nashville and Jacksonville, FL posted the fastest year-over-year 
growth among major metro areas, while New York and Washington DC saw the biggest decrease in median payment growth, 
according to Bank of America payments data. 
• Limited supply may continue to constrain access to child care for some households. Child care employment fell 0.7% year-over-
year in September, suggesting provider capacity remains under pressure and could continue to affect household finances and 
labor force participation.

Child care costs outpace rent and medical services 
Child care remains a significant expense for many families. In September, the growth in child care and other school-related fees 
surpassed other major household expenses such as rent and medical services, according to the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) 
(Exhibit 1). 
Yet, while child care prices continue to rise in official inflation data, median household payments have largely moderated since 
February, according to Bank of America data (Exhibit 2). More importantly, the growth in the number of households paying for 
child care has fallen since the start of 2025. 
Exhibit 1: The cost of child care has surpassed other services such as 
rent in the consumer price index

changing the amount of care they use, which is reflected in slower growth in the number paying for child care. As more

households shift into lower-cost care (school-age, public pre-K, subsidized or free after-school programs), median payments can

The decline in the number of households paying for child care may also reflect families seeking alternatives to formal care 
arrangements. Some may be relying more heavily on relatives, shifting parents’ work schedules or dropping out of the workforce 
altogether to care for their children in response to elevated costs. This could explain why, among all income groups paying for 
care, the number of lower-income households has seen the biggest decline, falling 2.0% year-over-year (YoY) in September 
(Exhibit 3). 
Exhibit 3: The number of lower-income households paying for child care has fallen the most, down 2.0% YoY in September

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
More families face larger monthly bills 
Yet even as fewer households pay for child care, the share of those shelling out more than $2,000 per month has increased 
substantially (Exhibit 4). In fact, it has more than doubled since 2023, reaching 7.7% in 2026 year-to-date. Meanwhile, the share 
of those paying $1,000 or less has declined, suggesting higher monthly payments are becoming more common even as the 
national median grows more slowly. 
Exhibit 4: The share of households paying >$2000 a month for child care has more than doubled since 2023 
Monthly child care payments by amount (annual average, share of households, %)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Rising costs take a bigger bite out of households’ paychecks 
What makes child care “affordable”? According to the U.S. Department of Health and Human Services, child care is considered 
affordable if it accounts for no more than 7% of family income. Yet, in Bank of America data, the average child care cost was 8% 
in September.  
While an 8% share may not appear dramatically above the federal affordability benchmark of 7%, it represents an average across 
households’ income levels. For families in high-cost metro areas or those paying more than $2,000 per month, the burden is 
likely considerably higher.

Where child care costs are climbing 
Child care pressures also differ substantially by location. In Bank of America payments data, Nashville posted the fastest YoY 
median payment growth in September, followed by Jacksonville, FL and Phoenix (Exhibit 6). This dispersion of growth across the 
country suggests child care cost pressures are no longer confined to historically expensive coastal markets.  
Interestingly, New York and Washington DC saw the sharpest decline in growth this year. Why might this be? The supply side of 
the market may help explain why – for example, New York City recently introduced universal Pre-K.1

Fewer providers, higher costs 
As of September 2026, employment in child care services was down 0.7% from a year earlier, suggesting the sector’s workforce 
recovery has stalled. Fewer workers can constrain the number of available places or the hours providers offer. That may leave 
families facing longer searches, fewer workable arrangements or continued price pressure. In turn, access to care can influence 
parents’ ability to enter, remain in or expand their participation in the workforce. 
Exhibit 7: Employment within child care services fell 0.7% YoY in September 
Employees by sector (monthly, seasonally adjusted, YoY%)

Source: Haver Analytics  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
What are the economic implications of rising child care costs? 
Child care costs may have important labor market implications for families with young children. Of those households paying for 
child care in 2026, more than half of them were Older Millennials (52%), followed by Younger Millennials (20%) and Gen X (16%), 
according to Bank of America payments data. This means child care costs weigh more heavily on Older Millennials. 
The share of Older Millennial households receiving more than one paycheck has continued to grow from a year earlier, but the 
pace of that improvement has moderated over the past three months (Exhibit 8). Given that this group accounts for the majority 
of households paying for child care in Bank of America payments data, they may offer early signs of how affordability affects 
their employment decisions. 
Exhibit 8: The share of Older Millennial households paying for child care that have more than one person with a paycheck has declined since 2021  
Share of households with a child care payment with more than one person with a paycheck (%, monthly, three-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Child care challenges can weigh on women’s employment 
According to data from the American Time Use Survey, women continue to perform significantly more child care and household 
work than men, even as their participation in paid work has increased. This suggests that persistent gaps in access and 
affordability may have disproportionate consequences for women’s employment. Indeed, the percentage of women who cited 
child care as a reason for temporary unemployment was nearly three times higher than men’s in 2026, though the average fell 
from 2025, according to data from the BLS. 
Yet labor force participation data indicates a more nuanced picture. Despite persistent child care pressures, mothers are 
participating in the workforce at rates that remain above their 2019 averages.

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Bank of America credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card holders 
making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards is excluded. Data regarding 
merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial services 
companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS), 
which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
If applicable, the consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level.  
If applicable, any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a 
deposit account or credit card. Any reference to aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-
peer, cash and checks.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

## Document 393: Persistent energy pressures have further to run (2026-11-04)

Persistent energy pressures have further to run
19 October 2026
Key points
• Shipping disruptions, depleted
inventories and elevated freight
costs are keeping physical crude
and fuel markets tight despite a

reported recovery in Gulf exports.
• Brent is forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing
to US$77/bbl in Q4 2027.
• Refined product markets are
expected to ease more slowly than
crude, with elevated gasoil cracks,
shipping and insurance costs slowing
declines in petrol and diesel prices.
• Under an escalation scenario,
renewed conflict and deeper supply
disruptions could push Brent towards
US$150/bbl in H1 2027, prolonging
elevated fuel and LNG prices.

• Under an early resolution scenario, a
durable US-Iran agreement accelerates
supply recovery, taking Brent towards
US$65/bbl by Q4 2027 and bringing
fuel and LNG prices down faster.

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Low inventories and higher shipping costs will keep oil and
fuel prices elevated, with Brent futures forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing to US$77/bbl by Q4 2027
as supply recovers. Refined fuels and LNG prices should

ease more slowly. Risks are two-sided: escalation could
push Brent towards US$150/bbl, while an early US-Iran
agreement could see a faster normalisation in supply with
prices returning close to pre-conflict levels by end-2027.
This article was first published in the October Market Outlook .
Brent crude is back above US$100/bbl on fading confidence
in a lasting US-Iran agreement and intermittent attacks on
shipping. Buffers remain depleted, with the IEA estimating

global observed inventories were 507mb lower at the end of
August than at the start of the conflict.
This comes despite reported crude exports through the Strait of
Hormuz recovering to around 70% to 90% of pre-conflict levels
in late September. Saudi and UAE exports through the Yanbu and
Fujairah pipelines continue, with the East-West pipeline back to
normal after the early September attacks. Tanker crossings have
risen, although transponders remain switched off, leaving visible

transit at 1% to 4% of pre-conflict levels, while cargo transfers in
the Gulf of Oman have increased as an alternative route.
However, these workarounds are slower and more costly. Ship-
to-ship transfers can take 5 to 10 days, US escorts through
the Gulf via a route close to the Oman shoreline cause delays
while some tankers are taking longer routes. Fewer ships are
also available, with many redeployed to other markets. Higher
insurance premiums are adding to costs, with Gulf-to-China

tanker rates above US$0.9mn a day, around 15x higher than a
year ago. With prompt barrels (available for physical delivery)
scarce, the gap between physical and futures prices widened
again, with Dated Brent around US$136/bbl in early October
against US$101/bbl for front-month futures.
Oil and fuel prices push higher ...
Refined prices have risen more than crude
Refined product markets were already tight going into the
conflict. Reduced Gulf supply, exacerbated by Ukrainian strikes

on Russian refineries, has added to that tightness. Together
with higher shipping costs and rising Dated Brent prices,
Singapore Gasoil 10ppm (diesel) has roughly doubled since the
Strait closed, compared with around 40% for crude futures.
Consequently, Singapore gasoil crack spread widened
to an average of around US$36.1/bbl in early October,
from US$17.5/bbl across 2025. Alongside the Australian
government’s excise tax returning to normal, after it had been

halved, this has lifted national diesel and petrol prices to
around A$2.83/L and A$2.41/L.
Outlook
We do not see a lasting US-Iran agreement until at least
mid-2027. In the meantime, we assume there will not be a
return to full-scale military intervention. However, periodic
Iranian attacks on ships and facilities are expected to continue,
as will the US blockade of Iranian exports, keeping a supply
risk premium in place. We look for Brent futures to average

around US$101/bbl in Q4, then ease gradually as demand
from refiners and governments to rebuild stocks partly offsets
recovering supply. As the premium fades and the market
moves into surplus in late 2027, we forecast Brent at around
US$77/bbl in Q4 2027, easing towards US$70/bbl in 2028 as
Middle East production fully recovers.
Oil balance to gradually improve over 2027
Global oil supply is expected to rise by 4.0mb/d (4.0%) to
103.7mb/d in 2027, following a 6.1% fall in 2026. Most of

this reflects a gradual recovery in Gulf output, with Houthi

Luka Belobajdic, Economist

3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
attacks on Red Sea shipping keeping Saudi production below
its pre-conflict level until H2 2027. Meanwhile, we look for UAE
production to continue to improve over 2027 and lift above
pre-conflict levels from 2028, as it is no longer constrained by

OPEC quotas. We do not expect a meaningful return of Iranian
oil until an agreement is reached, and damage sustained during
the conflict means production will take time to recover.
We expect global oil demand to be 103.7mb/d in 2026, 0.8mb/d
(0.7%) higher than in 2025, and to rise a further 1.9mb/d (1.8%)
to 105.6mb/d in 2027. Chinese crude imports will continue to
recover over the coming months as refiners restock ahead of
Lunar New Year, but we expect only a gradual return to pre-

conflict levels, as the electrification of transport limits the
recovery in Chinese demand. Globally, demand for diesel and jet
fuel recovers as transport and aviation activity picks up, although
some demand destruction and efficiency gains are expected
to be permanent. The conflict has also highlighted strategic
vulnerabilities, particularly across Asia, so we expect a push to
rebuild reserves adding around 0.5% to demand over 2027.
Fuel prices to ease more slowly than crude

Refined product supply is expected to recover more slowly
than crude, with Gulf refinery runs not returning to pre-war
levels until at least late 2027. Outside the Gulf, there has
been some constructive news. The US has lifted sanctions
on Russian diesel until April 2027, with Russia saying it will
supply more than 4Mt over the coming months, while China
has lifted its ban on refined product exports. However, we
are cautious about how much this will add to the market in

the short term. Much of the Russian supply is headed to the
US and will depend on Russia’s ability to restore refining and
export capacity, while doubts remain over the Russia-Ukraine
energy ceasefire announced by President Trump. China still
manages exports through quotas, which are likely to increase
only gradually as domestic stocks are rebuilt. Refinery runs
should lift ahead of Lunar New Year, but any increase in
exports will take time to reach regional markets.

With product supply recovering slowly and depleted inventories
being rebuilt, Singapore gasoil crack is likely to stay above its
long-run average even as Brent eases, narrowing from US$71/
bbl in Q4 to US$27/bbl by Q4 2027. With Singapore Gas 10ppm
prices, shipping and insurance costs to remain elevated and
easing only gradually in 2027, we forecast Australian diesel
to retail at an average of 274¢/L in Q4, easing to 200¢/L in Q4
2027, and petrol at 230¢/L and 187¢/L respectively.

LNG prices supported through 2027
Japanese LNG prices surged 15.2%mth in September,
averaging US$25.4/MMBtu as supply disruptions in Qatar
and its inability to divert exports around the Strait of Hormuz
tightened markets and intensified competition between
importers. While prices are now more than double pre-conflict
levels, demand destruction across Asia is providing some
offset to tight supply
Our Q4 forecast for Japanese LNG is now US$24.5/mmbtu and

we expect the market to remain structurally tight, with supply
recovery likely to be gradual and prices not returning to pre-
conflict levels until around 2028. Still, Australia’s domestic
gas market should remain insulated, supported by the FY2027
Federal Budget’s domestic gas reservation scheme, which
requires exporters to supply the domestic market with the
equivalent of up to 20% of gas exports. So far this year,
despite the increase in LNG spot prices in Asia, Australian

natural gas prices have remained under the $12 per GJ cap
that has proposed by the Commonwealth government. We are
expecting this to continue well into 2027.
Risks around our oil price outlook are two-sided
Talks are still ongoing in the background, with the US and
Iran exchanging proposals to reopen the Strait, but the gap
between them remains wide. The conflict has also expanded
to the Red Sea and, with inventories sharply drawn down,
there is a smaller buffer to absorb a further shock.

Using the Oxford Economics model, we assess an escalation
scenario, where energy markets face a deeper and more
persistent disruption as the conflict in the Middle East
intensifies. In this case, Brent futures rise to around US$150/
bbl in H1 2027, only dipping to below US$100/bbl in Q2 2028.
We also consider an early resolution scenario, where shipping
and Gulf supply normalise faster, returning Brent to near pre-
conflict levels by end-2027.
Escalation scenario

In the escalation scenario we assume:
Talks stall and the conflict escalates:  the gap between the
US and Iran proves too wide and the US resumes strikes after
the midterms. Iran responds with attacks on shipping and Gulf
energy infrastructure, damaging export, storage and refining
facilities. A lasting agreement is not reached until 2028.
Export routes are disrupted: more frequent attacks curtail
dark crossings of the Strait and ship-to-ship transfers in the

Gulf of Oman. Houthi attacks also cut off Saudi alternative
routes, forcing Gulf producers to cut output and Middle
East crude exports sharply lower. New routes and shipping
arrangements eventually support a recovery in Gulf flows, but
trade patterns take time to adjust.

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Product supply fails to recover:  The Russia–Ukraine energy
ceasefire does not hold and strikes on Russian refineries
continue, limiting how much diesel Russia can export,
even with the US sanctions waiver extended. China also

reintroduces its export ban to protect domestic supply.
Oil prices lift to around US$150/bbl in H1 2027: With little
buffer to absorb the supply loss, competition for physical
delivery intensifies, with physical prices well above futures
as prompt barrels remain scarce. Higher prices support
supply growth outside OPEC and drive demand destruction,
alongside further emergency stock releases, but a deeper
and more prolonged global deficit remains. Brent stays above

US$100/bbl until mid-2028 as governments rebuild reserves.
The Singapore gasoil crack widens and, with freight and
insurance costs also higher, Australian diesel and petrol prices
average around 247 cpl and 231 cpl at end 2027. Japanese
LNG also remains elevated around US$23.2/MMBtu in Q4
2027, $9.1 above our baseline.
Early resolution scenario
In the early resolution scenario we assume:
A durable agreement: The US and Iran agree terms to reopen
the Strait by the end of year, and, unlike the June interim deal,

the agreement holds, with progress towards a permanent
settlement in early 2027. Attacks on shipping cease and the
US blockade is lifted, allowing Iranian oil back into the market,
initially from storage. Vessels are quickly repositioned to the
Gulf, with freight and war-risk insurance easing from early
2027 rather than mid-year.
A faster recovery in supply:  With export routes secure, Gulf
production and damaged refinery and export capacity recover

sooner than in the baseline. Outside the Gulf, the US extends
its waiver on Russian diesel beyond April 2027 and China
raises export quotas as domestic stocks are rebuilt.
Demand settles below pre-conflict trend: Some demand
destruction and efficiency gains persist, particularly in China.
With inventories rebuilding faster, government restocking is
smaller and more gradual than in the baseline.
Prices fall quickly, but restocking provides a floor:  Brent

Brent futures ease to around US$88/bbl in Q1 2027 as the
premium for prompt physical barrels unwinds. As the market
moves into balance, Brent eases to around US$65/bbl by Q4
2027, close to pre-conflict levels.
Refined product prices ease faster on lower freight costs with
the Singapore gasoil crack narrowing. Australian diesel and
petrol prices fall to around 182 cpl and 170 cpl by Q4 2027. Gas
markets also ease, although damage to Qatar’s gas facility means

export capacity recovers gradually. Japanese LNG falls to around
US$11.9/MMBtu by end-2027, $2.1 below our baseline.
Economic impact
In the escalation scenario, higher fuel costs flow through to
transport, food and other energy-intensive goods such as
plastics and fertilisers. Australian headline CPI remains above
4.5% for much of 2027, with underlying inflation remaining
sticky at above 3% until late 2028. The RBA faces a difficult
trade off between much higher inflation and a significant

hit to activity, as households cut discretionary spending and
businesses defer investment. Higher LNG export earnings
provide only a moderate offset. In the early resolution scenario,
headline inflation is around 0.1ppt below the baseline in 2027.
New Zealand is more exposed in both scenarios, with food
and energy a larger share of its CPI and no offsetting energy
exports. Headline inflation is around 1ppt higher in the
escalation scenario in Q1 2027.

## Document 407: SemiAnalysis拆解算力荒：OpenAI与Anthropic正在抢光全球新增算力

Source PDF: all report pdf/SemiAnalysis拆解算力荒：OpenAI与Anthropic正在抢光全球新增算力.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SemiAnalysis 创始⼈ Patel 称， OpenAI 和 Anthropic 预订了新增算⼒的 25% 到 40% 。 “ 现在
对很多⼈来说，已经不可能买到算⼒了。 ” 此外，利率上⾏使 Neocloud 难借到钱，英伟达只
给约 6.5 吉⽡托底，占⽐有限，算⼒的交付权仍向超⼤规模云⼚商和两家实验室集中。
摘要：
11 ⽉ 3 ⽇，半导体研究机构 SemiAnalysis 创始⼈兼 CEO Dylan Patel 在 Runtime 2026 上发表演
讲，主题是算⼒、资本，以及谁能真正把算⼒交付出去。
他表⽰， OpenAI 和 Anthropic 正在⼤量预订算⼒，以 2027 年为例，两家拿⾛的新增算⼒占⽐在
25% 到 40% 之间。在需求挤压下，英伟达 H100 数据中⼼芯⽚的⼀年期合约价，已从去年 10 ⽉低
点的每⼩时 1.70 美元涨到 2.80 美元左右。对其他公司来说，买得起算⼒，不等于拿得到算⼒。
资⾦端同样吃紧：近两个⽉美债收益率⼤幅上升， 10 年期收益率 10 ⽉初已升⾄ 5% 以上，靠借钱

建机房的新兴云⼚商越来越难融到资。
这场演讲发⽣在 Anthropic 公开递交 S-1 的⼀周后，市场刚刚看到⼀家头部实验室背后的算⼒账。
Patel 说，英伟达已经出⾯为约 6.5 吉⽡的算⼒托底，但在 2030 年前的新增算⼒⾥，这只占很⼩⼀
部分、 “ 杯⽔⻋薪 ” 。
新增算⼒被两家实验室订⾛⼀⼤块
算⼒紧张的起点在买家太集中。全球算⼒总量已经涨得很快： 2024 年底约 60 吉⽡，今年底超过
100 吉⽡，两年接近翻倍。 Patel 说：
增量中最⼤的买家是 OpenAI 和 Anthropic 。两家⼿⾥的算⼒总量，去年底合计约 4 吉⽡，今年约
10 吉⽡，明年超过 20 吉⽡。拆成增量看， 2027 年⼀年⾥两家合计新增约 12 吉⽡，⽽全球当年新
增总量约 40 吉⽡，占⽐约三成。到 2028 年， Patel 称 AI 实验室⼀年就要新增约 30 吉⽡。
Patel 估算：
“2024 年底全球有 60 吉⽡的算⼒，到今年年底已经超过 100 吉⽡，两年时间⼏乎翻了⼀番。 ”

### Page 2

还有⼀个细节值得注意：这些算⼒主要⽤于训练，⽽不是⽤来服务⽤⼾（推理）。 Patel 说，
Anthropic 今天有 4 吉⽡，整个 Claude Code 只⽤其中约 1 吉⽡来服务，花在推理上的算⼒⽐训练
“ 少得惊⼈ ” 。这意味着，即便⽤⼾端的需求稳定下来，实验室训练新模型的预算仍会继续占⽤芯
⽚。
卖开源模型推理太赚钱，租价被⼀路抬⾼
买家多了，价格会涨。 Patel 认为，推动租价的另⼀股⼒量是卖 token （模型输出的计量单位）
这⻔⽣意本⾝利润极⾼，使得愿意⾼价租 GPU 的⼈远不⽌两家实验室。
他给出的账是按每兆⽡算的。⾃建⼀个 B300 集群，把建设和五年运营摊下来，约合每年 900 万
美元；向云⼚商租， “ 能做到每年 1400 万美元就算你⾛运 ” 。另⼀头，⽤这⼀兆⽡的算⼒去跑开源
模型 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash ，在利⽤率 60% 的情况下，⼀年的收⼊约 7000 万美元。也就是说，
哪怕租价⾼出⾃建成本五成，卖推理的收⼊仍是租⾦的五倍。（ 7000 万美元是收⼊，未扣除⼈⼒

和其他成本。）他说：
这个测算基于 SemiAnalysis 开源的 InferenceX 基准，⽤的是任何⼈都能下载的容器，只⽤了
vLLM 和 SGLang 两套开源推理软件，团队 “ 只有四个⼯程师 ” ，没有做投机解码等进⼀步优化。
价格数据与这套逻辑吻合。Patel 说，去年 10 ⽉是低⾕， H100 约 1.70 美元，最低能找到 1.45 美
元；现在成交价在 2.40 ⾄ 3.20 美元之间，中间值 2.80 美元：
“ 他们拿⾛的是新增算⼒⾥ 25% 、 30% 、 40% 的那⼀部分。 ”
“ 去年底， OpenAI 和 Anthropic 的收⼊随着 Claude Code 开始飙升，于是他们疯狂买算⼒，
Meta 也开始疯狂买。 ”
“ 现在对很多⼈来说，已经不可能买到算⼒了。 ”
“ 即使你说利⽤率只有 30% ，你仍然会赚到离谱的钱。 ”
“ 我认识的好⼏⼗个⼈，⽣意就是租⼀些 GPU 然后卖推理，赚了很多钱。 ”
“ 两年前你要是签这个价，会被当成疯⼦，现在⼈们急着在这个区间签约。 ”

### Page 4

Oracle 和 Google 也被评为很好，其中 Google 从⾸轮的铜牌⼀路升级。下调的则更多：随着评测
标准加严， Azure 降为银牌， Together 和 AWS 降为铜牌， Crusoe 也被下调。 AWS 的问题偏体
验：
评测⾥最刺眼的是安全。 SemiAnalysis 让 AI 模型去攻击这些云：
其中⼀次，他们⽤⼀个 “ 落后于 OpenAI 和 Anthropic” 的开源模型，拿到了整个集群的 Slurm （常
⽤的集群调度系统）权限，看到某国国防⼯业和国家安全情报机构的作业名称。他们随即停⽌
测试，并发表了《⼤多数 Neocloud 在安全上很烂》。另⼀项测试是向 GPU 注⼊故障（他提到的
Nvidia 错误码 XID 719 ），观察云⼚商多久发现。结果是：
他把这称为最低限度的测试： GPU 必然会出问题，云⼚商应当靠⾃⼰的监控发现并修复。
利率上⾏，新玩家借不到钱，英伟达托底也只是⼀⼩块
增加供给需要资本，⽽资本的价格正在上升。 Patel 重申，到 2029 年底全球 AI 资本开⽀约 11 万亿

美元，其中约 7 万亿来⾃债务。⼤部分钱花在芯⽚上，其余是数据中⼼、能源，以及为了更快投
产⽽在现场⾃建的电⼚。
在这样的利率环境下，贷款流向呈现出明显的偏向。 10 年期美债收益率今年从 3.96% 升到 10 ⽉
初的 5.31% ， AI 相关债务发⾏ 9 ⽉明显降温。 Patel 描述了⼀家有 100 兆⽡规模的 Neocloud 想扩到
1 吉⽡时的处境：
“ 它们未来六个⽉不会再卖算⼒给你了，已经售罄。 Nebius 会让你预付整整⼀年，或者六个⽉，
价格⾼得离谱，但这是从他们那⾥拿到算⼒的唯⼀办法。 ”
“ 要拿到⼀个集群，你得和⼤概⼗个⼈打交道，⽽且耗时极⻓，⽹络也有点难搞。 ”
“ 有些漏洞是互联⽹上早已公开的，模型能直接找到并利⽤，然后访问其他客⼾的存储。 ”
“ 很多云要等到你提醒了才会发现，有些即便你提醒了，也要⼏天才响应。 ”

### Page 5

结果是，明年已签约的算⼒⾥， “ ⼤约 65% 到 70%” 流向超⼤规模云⼚商和 AI 实验室。对创业公司
来说，租集群要先付⼀半，三到九个⽉后才能交付：
英伟达的做法是给集群托底， Patel 归纳为三类：
SoftBank Energy ： 只担保⼟地、电⼒和⼚房。这是俄亥俄州⼀座 9 吉⽡的设施，约 2030 年
投运，全部预留给 OpenAI 。
Firmus ： 英伟达承诺按某价格租下集群，使对⽅ “ 不赚钱但也不亏钱 ” ，但有了这份保证，别
⼈就愿意放贷。超出该价格的每⼀美元每⼩时，英伟达拿⾛⼀半， Patel 说 “ 很聪明，我喜
欢 ” 。
残值担保： 他只提到有这⼀类，没有展开。
规模上，英伟达已托底约 6.5 吉⽡，这部分会卖给开放市场⽽⾮只供实验室和超⼤规模云⼚商。
他给出的上限是 45 吉⽡， “ 即便如此也只占 2030 年前新增算⼒的很⼩⼀部分 ” 。他由此认为，
Fireworks 、 Modal 等做推理的公司，以及不少新实验室，正转向⾃建集群，拿贷款、找英伟达

背书。
演讲最后，他给出的判断是 token 的规模：⼏家提供商加起来每天处理超过 100 万亿 token ，各家
年化收⼊都在攀升。他的结语是：
“ 谁会借钱给他们？唯⼀的办法是把那⼀吉⽡卖给超⼤规模云⼚商，再由超⼤规模云⼚商去债市
融资，你拿到了贷款，但这些算⼒没有⼀点能进⼊⾯向整个⾏业的市场。 ”
“ 去找任何⼀家最近融到⼤钱的新实验室，对它们来说拿算⼒就是地狱。 ”
“ 希望他们能拿到算⼒，否则我们都是 Anthropic 和 OpenAI 的奴⾪。 ”
